(65)

which is essentially Gauss’ law in Bondi coordinates. The leading component in a large-
r
expansion reads

-\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\mathchoice{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}-\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{F_{{{A}{u}}}}{F_{{{A}{u}}}}{F_{{{A}{u}}}}{F_{{{A}{u}}}}=4\pi\mathchoice{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}},

(66)

where
\mathchoice{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}
is the
1/r^{2}
component of
\mathchoice{j_{{{u}}}}{j_{{{u}}}}{j_{{{u}}}}{j_{{{u}}}}
.
If we integrate over
u
we find

\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}=-\Delta\mathchoice{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}-4\pi\int\mathchoice{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}\differential{u}.

(67)

We thus see the memory tensor is associated to a shift in the Coulombic field—known as ordinary memory
7
—and to the escape of electric current through null infinity—known as null memory
7
.

Because
\mathcal{F}
is a closed form (
\differential\mathcal{F}=0
), its components obey the equation

\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}=2\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scr c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{]}{u}}}},

(68)

where
\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}
is the Levi-Civita connection on the unit sphere. This is essentially Faraday’s law written in Bondi coordinates and covariant notation. If we integrate both sides, we find (at leading order in a large-
r
expansion)

\left.\mathchoice{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}\right|_{+\infty}-\left.\mathchoice{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}\right|_{-\infty}=2\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{B}}}}\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{]}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{]}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{]}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{]}}}}.

(69)

The left-hand side poses an obstruction for the memory tensor to be a closed one-form. We will assume—as done by
7
;
68
—that
\mathchoice{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}{F_{{{A}{B}}}}
vanishes for
u\to\pm\infty
. This is indeed the case for the Liénard–Wiechert solution, and thus for an arbitrary superposition of massive electric charges. Massless charges are still allowed in the spacetime, as long as they are constrained to finite values of
u
(which is often the case of interest). With this assumption, we conclude that

2\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{[}{A}}}}\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}{]}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}{]}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}{]}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}{]}}}}=0,

(70)

meaning
\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}
is a closed one-form on the sphere. De Rham cohomology then tells us that
\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}
is necessarily exact. Therefore, there must exist a function
\varepsilon
on the sphere such that

\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}\varepsilon=\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}.

(71)

If we rewrite Eq. (
67
) in terms of
\varepsilon
, we find

\mathcal{D}^{2}\varepsilon=-\Delta\mathchoice{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}{F_{{{u}{r}}}}-4\pi\int\mathchoice{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}{j^{{{(2)}}}_{{{u}}}}\differential{u},

(72)

where
\mathcal{D}^{2}=\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}
is the Laplacian on the sphere. This is the Poisson equation on the sphere. It can be solved as long as the right-hand side has vanishing average on the sphere. This is the case, because we already know the right-hand side is the divergence of a vector field. The Laplacian
\mathcal{D}^{2}
can then be inverted and
\varepsilon
is uniquely defined.

IV.2
The asymptotic symmetries

Let us now consider electrodynamics in terms of the four-potential. Generically, we have

\mathcal{F}=\differential\mathcal{A}.

(73)

We can always choose to work in Lorenz gauge, in which case the four-potential has to obey

\differential{\star}\mathcal{A}=0.

(74)

Within Lorenz gauge, there is a residual gauge symmetry given by

\mathcal{A}\to\mathcal{A}+\differential\lambda,

(75)

where
\lambda
is required to satisfy

\differential{\star}\differential\lambda=0.

(76)

This is the wave equation for
\lambda
.

We are not interested in arbitrary field configurations, but rather in configurations with the falloffs (
61
). To achieve them, we impose that the potential obeys

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}}{r}+\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\lambda+o\quantity(\frac{1}{r}),

(77a)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}}{r^{2}}+\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}\lambda+o\quantity(\frac{1}{r^2}),

(77b)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}+\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}\lambda+o\quantity(1),

(77c)

where
\lambda
is a scalar function—a gauge parameter—with the expansion

\lambda(u,r,\mathchoice{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})\sim\sum_{k=0}\frac{\lambda^{(k)}}{r^{k}}+\sum_{k=1}\frac{\hat{\lambda}^{(k)}\log{r}}{r^{k}}.

(78)

These choices of falloffs impose boundary conditions on the configurations we are interested in. They define what is the phase space for the theory. See, for example, Refs.
24
;
76
. The choice to include looser falloffs in the pure gauge section (i.e., the choice of allowing
\varepsilon
to enter the falloffs for
\mathcal{A}
) is a necessity of Lorenz gauge
36
;
20
;
80
. An analysis in radial gauge—which was preferred in Refs.
43
;
85
;
68
, for example—would not need logarithmic contributions, but it would lead to difficulties when generalizing to a two-form theory
35
.

In Bondi coordinates, the wave equation satisfied by
\lambda
can be written as

-\frac{2}{r}\partialderivative{r}\quantity(r \tensor{\partial}{_u}\lambda)+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partialderivative{r}\quantity(r^2 \tensor{\partial}{_r}\lambda)+\frac{\mathcal{D}^{2}\lambda}{r^{2}}=0.

(79)

In terms of the asymptotic expansion, we obtain

2(n-1)\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\lambda^{(n)}-2\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\hat{\lambda}^{(n)}-(2n-3)\hat{\lambda}^{(n-1)}\\
+(\mathcal{D}^{2}+(n-1)(n-2))\lambda^{(n-1)}=0

(80a)

and

2(n-1)\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\hat{\lambda}^{(n)}+(\mathcal{D}^{2}+(n-1)(n-2))\hat{\lambda}^{(n-1)}=0.

(80b)

For
n=0
, we find

\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\lambda^{(0)}=0,

(81)

and thus the leading term is time-independent. For
n=1
, we find

2\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\hat{\lambda}^{(1)}=\mathcal{D}^{2}\lambda^{(0)},

(82)

which determines
\hat{\lambda}^{(1)}
from
\lambda^{(0)}
and an initial condition
\hat{\lambda}^{(1)}(-\infty,\mathchoice{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})
. Notice
\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\lambda^{(1)}
is left unspecified. For
n>1
, the equations can be solved hierarchically in terms of initial data for each
\lambda^{(n)}
and
\hat{\lambda}^{(n)}
and knowledge of the previous coefficients.

Most importantly, we notice the existence of residual gauge transformations—i.e., gauge transformations preserving Lorenz gauge—that do not vanish at infinity. These transformations have the asymptotic behavior

\lambda(u,r,\mathchoice{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})=\varepsilon(\mathchoice{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})+\order{\frac{\log{r}}{r}}.

(83)

Since
\varepsilon(\mathchoice{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})
remains finite at infinity, these are known as large gauge transformations.

Now let us connect this discussion to memory. Because the memory tensor
\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}
is an exact form, it can be understood as a gauge transformation. To make this concrete, let us write it in terms of the four-potential. In any globally defined gauge, we can write

\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}=\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}-\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}.

(84)

At leading order,

\mathchoice{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{u}}}}=-\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80 (85)

where we assume
\mathcal{A}
to fall off as in Eq. (
77
). Notice the memory tensor is given by

\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}=-\Delta\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}.

(86)

Hence, since
\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}
is exact,

\Delta\mathchoice{A_{{{A}}}}{A_{{{A}}}}{A_{{{A}}}}{A_{{{A}}}}=-\mathchoice{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}\varepsilon.

(87)

In other words, the difference between the late and early time values of the potential
\mathchoice{A^{{{(1)}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{{(1)}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{{(1)}}}_{{{A}}}}{A^{{{(1)}}}_{{{A}}}}
is an exact form on the sphere.

What is the physical meaning of this? Suppose that at early times (
u\to-\infty
) there is no radiation in the spacetime. At this time, we choose a gauge and define that the vacuum—the absence of electromagnetic radiation—is to be understood as
\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}=0
.

During intermediate times, radiation is emitted to infinity. For example, charges get accelerated and radiate. These bursts of radiation will generically change the value of the Coulombic field, because the final configuration will involve charges with different velocities than the initial configuration. At late times, we are once again in the absence of radiation. However, due to the memory effect, we no longer have
\mathchoice{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\mathcal{A}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}=0
. Instead, the potential for a vacuum (i.e., radiation-free) configuration is now a gauge transformation away, in accordance with Eq. (
87
). The definition of what is a vacuum has changed. The natural Fock spaces for quantum electrodynamics at early and late times are also inequivalent—see Refs.
80
;
73
for thorough discussions.

Importantly, the memory tensor can be written as a large gauge transformations. More specifically, we can always write

\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}=\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}\lambda+o(1).

(88)

This is true because
\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}}}}=\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}}}}
will vanish, but the leading-order behavior of
\lambda
is independent of both
u
and
r
. Hence, at leading order, the memory in the electromagnetic field is given precisely by a large gauge transformation. Equation (
72
) tells us how to choose a large gauge transformation (based on its leading behavior
\varepsilon
) that “resets” the potential to zero at late times.

IV.3
The soft theorem

Soft theorems are arguably the best understood corner of the infrared triangle, and thus we will merely sketch their relation to the rest of the triangle.

To further understand the large gauge transformations transformations, one can work in the covariant phase space
56
;
48
;
49
;
93
;
5
;
39
;
24
;
34
and define the canonical charges generating these transformations. Imposing that these charges commute with the
S
-matrix constrains different scattering amplitudes in the theory, leading to the soft theorem
43
;
19
. See, for example, Ref.
85
for a detailed discussion.

In turn, it is possible to use quantum field theoretic scattering amplitudes to obtain information about classical observables
52
;
25
;
51
;
64
. The soft theorem gives information about the low-energy behavior of photons, which, when Fourier transformed, is mapped into a long-distance behavior, recovering the memory effect. See also the discussions in Refs.
80
;
73
.

V
Memory in the Two-Form Formalism

Armed with a thorough understanding of the Maxwell infrared triangle, let us now move back to linear acoustics in the gauge-theoretic formulation. We will derive the memory effect in terms of the two-form potential, and then move on to relate it to asymptotic symmetries.

Memory effects can be classified as linear, nonlinear, ordinary, and null
8
;
10
;
80
. “Linear” and “nonlinear” refer to the original calculations in general relativity, in which the memory effect was first identified in a linearized analysis
100
;
14
;
15
and a nonlinear counterpart was understood much later
23
;
11
;
88
. “Ordinary” and “null” correspond to the two pieces given in Eq. (
67
). As highlighted by
7
;
8
, the null contribution is due to massless fields that reach infinity.

In order to discuss null memory in acoustics, we would need to include sources in the equations of motion that can reach null infinity. On the one hand, this is physically reasonable. In fact, in acoustics, it is very reasonable to consider supersonic sources, which could lead to a new type of “supersonic memory.” We will not pursue this route here, both for simplicity and because such an analysis seems more natural once nonlinear effects in fluid flow are also being taken into account. As a consequence, we also ignore any sorts of null memory, and focus instead on the case of (linear) ordinary memory. This will already allow us to investigate connections to the other corners of an acoustic infrared triangle.

From this point onward, we settle for simplicity and take the background medium to be homogeneous and quiescent, which means the acoustic metric (
27
) is the Minkowski metric up to coordinate redefinitions. This assumption seems analogous to the choice of working with Maxwell electrodynamics in a flat background. We assume units have been chosen so that
\rho_{0}=c=1
, which means the problem has effectively been reduced to studying the dynamics of a two-form field in a flat spacetime. As mentioned in the introduction, aspects of this problem have been investigated by other authors before with different motivations
29
;
30
;
1
;
2
;
33
;
36
;
44
;
58
;
59
;
75
.

As in the electromagnetic case, we equip flat spacetime with Bondi coordinates, which brings the Minkowski metric into the form (
57
). In these coordinates, the components of the three-form
\mathcal{H}
are

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}{j}{k}}}}{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}{j}{k}}}}{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}{j}{k}}}}{\mathcal{H}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c \displaystyle=-\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\script (97)

Notice
\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625p 1/r^{2}
. Integrating, we obtain

(99)

This result matches Eq. (
22
) at leading order, which was obtained in terms of
\phi
and using standard hydrodynamics techniques. The possible differences at subleading orders are due to our general ansatz in Eq. (
93
), which does not assume
\mathcal{H}
to be expanded in a power series in
1/r
.

Equation (
98
) plays a key role. Notice it critically depends on the interpretation of the two-form field as a dual description of linear acoustics. It is not a prediction from “ab initio” two-form electrodynamics. In fact, the natural coupling of a two-form field would be to a string, not to a point particle. This is how the Kalb–Ramond field finds its way into string theory
72
;
6
;
57
. Furthermore, a string memory effect has already been obtained in connection with the two-form theory
2
. Very importantly, however, Eq. (
98
) states we are considering how the fluid particles are affected by the dynamics of the two-form field, which ensures we are still analyzing an acoustic problem.

Under the light of the electromagnetic analysis, Eq. (
98
) invites us to define a memory tensor through

(100)

where

. Notice other components of
\mathcal{H}
do not enter the memory effect at leading order, and thus
\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}{\mu}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}{\mu}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}{\mu}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.72806pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80504pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0036pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{u}{\mu}}}}=\mathchoice{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{r}{\mu}}}}{\Delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\tex . As in the electromagnetic case, the memory tensor cannot depend on
u
or
r
, and it is a differential form on the sphere.

There is, however, an important difference at this point. For the acoustic two-form theory, the memory tensor is a two-form. Since it is a form on the sphere, it is automatically closed, because all three-forms on the sphere vanish. This, however, does not imply

(108)

Therefore, the difference between
\mathchoice{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}
at late and early times is a (large) gauge transformation parameterized by forms on the sphere, in addition to a global shift by a closed, but nonexact, form. Notice that
\mathchoice{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}
is multiplied by
1/r
in the expression for
\mathcal{B}
. Hence, a closed, but nonexact, form in
\mathchoice{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}
is not a topological term in the full theory. In particular the constant
C
occurs in the expressions for
\mathcal{H}
, as we shall now see. A topological contribution such as what happens in the Aharonov–Bohm effect or such as the configuration alluded to in Sec.
III.1
would be unrelated to the constant
C
above.

The exact contributions are precisely what we would expect given our experience with electrodynamics. Let us investigate the nonexact term in more detail. We see that

\Delta\mathchoice{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{B^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}=C\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}{B}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebo
